## Ultra Realistic Walk-in Closet Mirror Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Ultra realistic mirror selfie of a young woman in her early 20s sitting cross-legged on a polished wooden floor inside a luxury walk-in closet. She has shoulder-length dark hair with {argument name="hair color" default="neon green"} roots, natural makeup, soft neutral expression. Wearing an oversized cream knit cardigan sweater. Barefoot, relaxed casual pose. Holding a smartphone in front of a large full-length mirror with a wooden frame. Background features organized wooden shelves with neatly arranged shoes, handbags, folded clothes and hanging garments. Warm ambient recessed lighting, soft indoor glow, cinematic depth of field, highly detailed wood textures, authentic smartphone photography look, natural skin texture, realistic reflections, 4K ultra HD, photorealistic.",
  "style": "photorealistic",
  "aspect_ratio": "4:5",
  "quality": "ultra",
  "lighting": "warm indoor ambient",
  "detail_level": "maximum realism"
}
```

## Fitness Mirror Selfie Squat Pose Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

  },
  "camera_perspective": {
    "pov": "first person looking down at a laptop",
    "angle": "slightly above the keyboard looking at the screen",

      "type": "Athletic with realistic soft curves",

    "pose": {

## Nostalgic 90s Disposable Camera Snapshot

> Retro görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A young woman with the same unchanged face, pale soft complexion, caught in a classic 90s disposable-camera snapshot. Loose black hair, calm relaxed look. She’s wearing a pastel lavender hoodie with a soft worn texture. Background cluttered with polaroids and taped photos. Gentle flash, soft nostalgic tone.
```

## Character Generation Prompt (Step 1)

> Minimal, lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
I. Composition & Spatial Layout
1.Framing & Scale: The subject occupies approximately 60–70% of the frame height. Leave at least 30% of the frame height as empty space above the head. Both left and right sides retain clearly visible empty wall space.
2.Visual Focus: A minimal, uncluttered background designed to strongly emphasize facial structure, eye expression, and skin quality.

II. Lighting & Shadow Style
1.Key Light: Intense natural daylight, side-backlit with a slight reverse angle, cool-toned, producing extreme high-contrast separation between highlights and shadows.
2.Light Characteristics: Dense, hard-edged leaf-shadow patterns projected across the wall and the subject’s body and face. Shadow edges are razor-sharp with extremely narrow penumbra.
3.Highlight Control: Large areas of the background are deliberately overexposed while retaining faint surface texture, creating a glowing, almost blinding spatial atmosphere.

III. Subject Appearance (Face & Hair — Reference Applied)
1.Facial Structure:
A young Japanese woman in her 20s with a refined V-shaped face, sharp jawline, and high-definition facial contours. Overall facial balance is elegant yet striking, with high visual impact.
2.Skin Quality:
Porcelain-toned Japanese skin with a cool undertone. Ultra-smooth, luminous texture enhanced by strong contrast lighting while retaining realistic skin detail.
3.Eyes & Expression:
・Large, sharp fox-shaped eyes
・Confident gaze directed straight into the camera
・Expression carries a cool, playful, slightly teasing energy
・K-pop idol–inspired eyeliner with clearly emphasized aegyosal
・Overall expression is controlled, calm, and subtly provocative rather than smiling
4.Mouth:
Relaxed lips with a subtle, effortless smirk. Glossy gradient lips with a refined sheen, never exaggerated.
5.Hair Style:
Long, sleek, straight black hair reaching the waist, with perfectly straight, neatly trimmed Japanese bangs. Hair finish is glossy, clean, and fashion-forward, moving minimally in the light.
6.Makeup Style:
High-contrast K-pop idol–inspired makeup. Clean base, sculpted facial structure, cool-toned color balance, no warm or heavy blush.
7.Accessories (Minimal):
・Small gold pendant necklace
・Small gold hoop earrings
・Minimalist tattoo on the left inner forearm

IV. Clothing
・Cropped pure-white bandeau-style tube top
・Body-hugging but not tight, exposing the waist
・Fabric remains clean and structured, no wrinkles distracting from silhouette
```

## Passport Photo Grid Generation

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "description": "Ultra-realistic passport-size photo of me (exact face likeness, 99% match, no facial changes).",
  "style": "Professional studio portrait suitable for official ID or passport use.",
  "outfit": "as per uploaded pic",
  "expression": "Neutral closed-lip smile, straight look, both ears visible, shoulders included.",
  "lighting": "Even balanced front lighting, no harsh shadows, soft studio tone.",
  "background": "Plain light cream or light blue background, clean and uniform.",
  "composition": "Centered head and shoulder framing with natural proportions as per passport standards.",
  "output": "Generate 6 identical passp ort/ticket-size photos (3x2 grid layout vertical) on one sheet, evenly spaced, print-ready, high-resolution studio quality."
}
```

## Dramatic Black & White Portrait

> Sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a professional promptet of a handsome young man with 100% unaltered facial features and hair, using a high-definition Full HD camera in 8K resolution. The aperture is 3:4. The young man appears at a narrow 30-degree angle, his upper body in black and white, looking down with his hand on his chest towards his heart. He wears a dark black wool sweater and a stylish, sporty, rubberized watch with a dark black bezel and hands, perfectly complementing the scene and his face. The lighting is very shallow and subdued, creating a dramatic effect against a dark black background, while preserving the original facial features and hair.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Passport Photo Generation

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a ultra-realistic passport-size photo of me using the uploaded image as the only identity reference, exact face likeness with 100% match and no facial changes. Professional studio portrait suitable for official ID or passport use. Outfit: white collar shirt and matching tie with branded modern style. Expression: neutral closed-lip smile, straight look at camera, both ears clearly visible, shoulders included in frame. Clean white studio background, even lighting with no shadows, sharp focus on facial features, natural skin texture, no beautification, no retouching, no stylization. Photorealistic, high resolution, official ID standard framing.
```

4:5 aspect ratio, 2K quality.
Photorealistic studio portrait.
Use the person from the attached reference photo. Keep the face, skin tone, hairstyle, and likeness exactly the same.

High contrast, intense, cinematic mood. Sharp focus on the face, smooth background.

## 4-Panel Studio Portrait Grid

```
A photorealistic 2x2 grid arranged in a 1:1 square format studio photograph of the man from image_0.png, wearing a black t shirt and a modern fade haircut against a white cyclorama background. The four panels show distinctly different angles:Top-Left Panel: A direct front headshot, looking straight at the camera.Top-Right Panel: A profile view where the man is turned, facing strictly towards the right edge of the frame, showing only the right side of his face.Bottom-Left Panel: A profile view where the man is turned in the opposite direction, facing strictly towards the left edge of the frame, showing only the left side of his face.Bottom-Right Panel: A rear view headshot, showing the back of his head, neck, and the details of the fade haircut.
```

## Amateur iPhone Photo Portrait with Accurate Face Reference

```
An amateur photo taken on an iPhone. Use the face from the uploaded photos as the ONLY and ACCURATE face reference keep the proportions of the face 1:1, accurate geometry, similarity, age and expression. Don't change your facial features by even 1%. No embellishments, no stylization. The scene: an outdoor parking lot near a low modern brick building, early evening soft sky, a few parked cars in the background, casual street atmosphere, slightly messy urban surroundings. Pose and action: the camera is slightly below eye level and close to the subject. The model is framed chest-up, centered, shoulders relaxed, body turned 3/4, head slightly tilted toward the camera, one hand lifting the brim of a {argument name="cap color" default="black"} baseball cap, the other arm relaxed out of frame. Outfit: oversized {argument name="sweatshirt color" default="light beige/cream"} sweatshirt with subtle worn texture, black baseball cap with small white stitching, no jewelry, no accessories. Emotions: a wide natural smile, friendly candid vibe. Lighting: soft natural daylight (late afternoon/early evening), gentle even shadows, no flash, natural contrast. Realistic skin: hyper-realistic skin texture, visible pores, barely noticeable imperfections, natural skin shine. Hair: natural growth line, a few stray hairs near the cap, realistic strands. Hands: natural skin texture, soft transitions of light on the knuckles, realistic nails. Camera and appearance: the photo was taken on an iPhone, amateur framing, slightly tilted horizon, RAW image, jpeg compression, moderate grain, natural color tones, no retouching, shallow depth of field. Ar 3:4.
```

```
Realistic indoor daytime smartphone photo, candid and unposed, captured like a random accidental click. FACE LOCK — HIGHEST PRIORITY Use the uploaded reference image as the only and exclusive facial identity source. Preserve the face exactly 1:1 with zero deviation: same identity same facial structure and proportions same skin tone same eyes, brows, nose, lips, jawline same hairline and facial hair No reinterpretation, no beautification, no regeneration, no face drift. Facial accuracy overrides lighting, pose, motion blur, and realism. POSE & BODY (LOCKED — EXACT) The subject is standing at the top landing / exit of an indoor escalator inside a modern shopping mall. Leaning against a glass railing with a wooden hand rest. Right elbow resting on the wooden railing Right hand touching the back of the head near the neck Head tilted downward Chin slightly tucked Face mostly hidden by falling hair Eyes looking toward the floor, not at the camera Shoulders slightly slouched Torso leaning subtly forward Legs relaxed, lightly crossed at the ankles The posture feels tired, introspective, accidental — not posed. OUTFIT (LOCKED — EXACT) Oversized off-white / cream T-shirt loose fit short sleeves very small, subtle chest text (not bold, not decorative) Light-wash baggy-fit jeans loose through the legs natural folds and creases Black-and-white low-top sneakers Black backpack worn on one shoulder, hanging naturally down the back single strap visible No jewelry. No accessories. No sunglasses. HAIR & SKIN Medium-length black hair Naturally messy, textured, lived-in Hair falling forward, partially covering the face Expression calm and unreadable Real skin texture with visible pores No smoothing, no beautification BACKGROUND (LOCKED) Modern shopping mall interior Glass railing with vertical metal supports Wooden handrail on top of the glass Escalator ending immediately beside the subject black escalator belt visible Clean white walls Minimal architectural design Neutral tiled flooring Open mall space visible behind the glass Quiet, spacious, real environment No decorative staging, no crowd emphasis LIGHTING (LOCKED) Soft indoor mall lighting Even illumination No flash No dramatic shadows Natural reflections on glass and metal Neutral white balance CAMERA & FRAMING Handheld smartphone photo Eye-level framing Full body visible Slight real-world imperfection No portrait mode No background blur No cinematic depth Natural sharpness with mild softness OVERALL FEEL A real, unplanned moment captured while pausing near an escalator. Quiet Gen-Z aesthetic. Minimal, human, Pinterest-worthy without trying. Believable and unstaged. NEGATIVE PROMPT studio lighting, cinematic grading, fashion posing, beauty filter, smooth skin, AI look, CGI, portrait blur, dramatic lighting, exaggerated expressions, oversharpening.
```

## AI Influencer Mirror Selfie Pose Prompt

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A "gritty" (raw/grainy) mirror selfie taken with an iPhone late at night in a female dormitory. The woman is captured through the reflection, casually holding the phone at chest height, partially obscuring her face. The mirror is slightly dirty, with smudges and dust residue that add texture and realism.

She is sitting on the edge of her bed, slightly leaning forward.

Her posture is relaxed and natural (unposed): one leg bent, the other hanging naturally, shoulders loose, and the body slightly angled due to the mirror's perspective. Head subtly tilted to the viewer's right, the face dominates almost the entire frame, shoulders rounded forward, and the body leaning slightly towards the camera to achieve that classic cozy night-time selfie compression.

The right hand (viewer's left side) is raised directly in front of the face, at chin or mouth level, with the palm clearly facing the camera, performing a prominent peace sign / V-sign gesture:

The index and middle fingers are fully extended and well separated (an angle of ~60–70° between them).

The ring and pinky fingers are fully bent, tightly pressed, and held flat by the thumb.

The thumb firmly anchors the bent fingers, being clearly visible as it presses against them.

The fingers point slightly upwards and forwards (not perfectly vertical, with a soft natural tilt).

Hand positioned centrally, very close to the face; the fingertips are approximately at the level of the lips or nose, creating a strong foreground element.

The left hand is not visible, completely out of frame.

Relaxed and casual torso posture: shoulders slightly hunched forward, neck gently stretched towards the lens, the typical hunched pose of a low-light bedroom selfie.

Atmosphere: intimate, playful, low-light night atmosphere, with a strong emphasis on the bold hand gesture in the center of the frame, just in front of the face.

She wears an elegant {argument name="top garment" default="fitted black crop-top"} with a sharp, refined silhouette, a {argument name="bottom garment" default="black leather mini-skirt"} and {argument name="footwear" default="white sneakers"}. She has a wild wavy hairstyle. The high fashion contrasts effortlessly with the relaxed atmosphere of the room.

The lighting comes solely from the bedroom ceiling lamp, raw and imperfect: direct phone flash reflected in the mirror, blown-out highlights on the skin and fabric, harsh shadows, uneven exposure, visible grain, slight motion blur, flash flares, and reflections within reflections.
```

## Moody urban portrait under a concrete overpass

> Sinematik, retro mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A moody, urban portrait of me standing under a desolate concrete overpass, the harsh architectural lines framing a lone figure. I'm wear ing an oversized white t-shirt and loose dark pants, hands in pockets, a relaxed posture. The ground is rough, textured concrete, with hints of dampness. Cinematic top lighting with deep shadows and rich tex ture, emphasizing the starkness of the environment.

Shot on 35mm, f/2.8, ISO 400, 1/125s. Apply Fuji Classic Chrome preset with warm highlights, strong contrast, slight desaturation, light film grain, and soft vignette for a nostalgic, non-blue tone look.
```

## Hyper-Fidelity Mirror Selfie with Identity Locking

> Fotogerçekçi moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(9:16). Portrait orientation. Raw high-fidelity photograph, iPhone 17 Pro simulation, 24mm wide-angle focal length, f/2.4 aperture for total structural depth, authentic digital grain, mirror-selfie perspective.

[Subject & Identity Locking]

Identity Lock: Absolute inheritance of all facial geometry, epidermal characteristics, ocular structure, and natural hair color/texture directly from Reference Image A. The AI must perform a 1:1 pixel-mapping of the subject's identity from Image A into the target scene.
Bio-Fidelity: Epidermal physics featuring visible micro-pores, fine vellus hair along the jawline, and satin-finish hydration on the T-zone, maintaining the exact skin-tone profile inherited from Image A.
Expression Sync: Expression Lock—replicate the mirror-selfie gaze through the lens, specific pouty lip tension, and soft tension of the facial muscles from Reference Image B.
[Outfit & Physicality] (CASE B - Standard Fashion)

Technical Description: High-tensile rib-knit mini-dress in {argument name="dress color" default="deep chocolate brown"} with a mock-neck collar and short sleeves. Hemline at upper-thigh geometry.
Textile Architecture: Visible vertical ribbing texture; material-to-skin tension showing realistic bunching at the waist. Paired with checkered-pattern comfort slides.
Anatomical Volume: Hyper-defined S-curve silhouette; maximum anatomical emphasis on the waist-to-hip ratio and leg definition; apply the 20% over-description rule for "Hyper-defined hourglass geometry" to counteract model bias.
Accessories: Transparent clear-frame optical glasses; long square-tip manicured nails; a small luxury monogram-texture leather handbag held in the left hand. Blue-cased smartphone held for the selfie.
[Pose & Interaction]

Geometry: Full-body standing mirror selfie pose. Right arm raised for the phone; left arm at the side holding the handbag. Subject is centered with a slight hip-pop to emphasize the silhouette.
Articulation: Precise finger-joint articulation on the smartphone and the bag handle; realistic skeletal alignment of the shoulders and neck.
[Environment & Lighting]

Atmosphere: Casual domestic interior apartment setting; soft diffused 4000K neutral-warm overhead lighting.
Background Integrity: Pixel-perfect inventory of wood-plank flooring; white interior doors (one featuring a {argument name="sticker detail" default="Hello Kitty sticker"}); a kitchen breakfast bar with a dark wooden bar stool; a pink handbag hanging in the mid-ground; and miscellaneous household items on the counter.
Shadow Ray-Tracing: Natural ambient occlusion in the garment folds; soft shadows cast by the subject onto the floor; realistic mirror-plane reflection with accurate depth and light-bounce from the background elements.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Couple Mirror Selfie Prompt

```
Ultra-realistic smartphone mirror selfie of a couple late 20s–early 30s, indoor near window, daytime. Close-up framing collarbone to top of head. Phone partially covering face, slight downward gaze, calm neutral expression. Natural tied-back dark hair with flyaways, light grey cotton hoodie with clear fabric texture separation, small silver earrings. Skin realism focus: visible pores, fine micro-texture, organic acne marks (non-repeating), uneven pigmentation, subtle redness, natural oil sheen only on high points. No symmetry correction. Real daylight with slight warmth, accurate white balance, natural contrast. True-to-life colors. Phone-camera realism, subtle sensor grain, slight edge softness. No retouching, no smoothing, no beauty filters. Negatives: AI glow, plastic skin, skincare-ad look, cinematic lighting, studio light, pastel or faded colors, flat lighting, cartoon/3D style, logos or text.
```

## Hyper-Expressive 3D Cartoon Portrait from Photo Reference

> Sinematik, minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Convert the input photo into a hyper-expressive 3D cartoon portrait in the exact style of the provided reference image, faithfully preserving the real facial features of the original person, including skin tone, eyebrows, lips, nose shape, beard, and overall bone structure, while subtly exaggerating proportions for a stylized cartoon look; eyes should be large and expressive, slightly bulging for comedic impact, and the face must show {argument name="expression" default="surprise or shock"} with a dramatically open mouth, thick glossy lips, and strong emotional intensity; skin must be realistically textured with visible pores and facial hair; include detailed accessories matching the input photo such as earrings, bandanas, or chains; lighting should be sharp and cinematic, featuring a dramatic key light from the left, soft fill from the right, and subtle rim lighting; use a clean or gradient background to keep focus on the character; maintain strict anatomical accuracy within a Pixar–Illumination hybrid visual language; render in high resolution with a clay-style material finish; final image must be in vertical 3:4 portrait aspect ratio.
```

  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",

      },

        "preserve_composition": true,
        "preserve_lighting": true,
        "preserve_palette": true,
        "preserve_vibe": true
      }
    },

    "global_constraints": {
      "rating": "PG-13",
      "no_nudity": true,
      "no_explicit_sexual_content": true,
      "no_logos": true,
      "no_watermark": true,
      "no_readable_text": true
    },

      "sharpness": "crisp_but_natural",

      "color_grade": "clean_daylight_neutral_warm_skin",
      "dynamic_range": "natural_not_hdr",

    },

      "scene_summary": "Create a photoreal close-up fashion selfie of one adult woman in clean indoor daylight. The woman has long, softly waved medium-brown hair with natural highlights, parted slightly off-center.\
\
Styling details (must match): {argument name=\"sunglasses type\" default=\"tortoiseshell cat-eye sunglasses\"} with dark lenses; soft glam makeup with natural brows, subtle contour, and full glossy rose-nude lips; a chunky gold hoop earring visible; layered pearl necklaces including a statement pendant charm. Outfit: light blue thin-striped button-up shirt, collar open, slightly oversized fit.\
\
Environment: bright modern interior with a white wall/tiles on one side, a wooden louvered door or panel behind, and a white cabinet/ceiling fixture in the background. Background should be softly out of focus (shallow depth of field).\
\
Lighting: natural window light, soft and even, with realistic reflections visible on the sunglasses lenses. Preserve real skin texture and pores; avoid smoothing or beauty retouching. No text, no logos,"
    }
  }
}

## Low-Light Bedroom Selfie Prompt for Nano Banana (Re-post)

```
Vertical frame, realistic low-light phone selfie, in a dark night bedroom, face identical to the reference image. Pale face with bluish-purple screen light; dark background with beige blackout curtains on the left. Sleepy and cozy late-night lo-fi atmosphere, close-up, slightly low angle. Subject lying on their stomach, leaning on a patterned gray pillow, messy black hair and thin bangs. Soft, dreamy, vacant gaze, mouth slightly open, hand near the face with fingers touching the lips. Light black sleeveless rash top, thin silver necklace, left shoulder exposed. Soft focus, visible grain, high contrast between the bright face and dark background. Negative prompt: bright daytime, sunlight, studio/flash lighting, warm/orange tones, outdoors, sharp focus, professional camera, distorted hands, extra/missing fingers, out-of-focus face, only one photo.
```

## 3-Frame Film Collage Prompt (Photographer Version)

> Sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the attached face photo as is to create a vertical 3-frame low-res webcam collage.

Absolute rules regarding the face:

Do not alter the face at all

Maintain the exact proportions of features, face shape, eye size, nose, mouth, and jawline

Do not make the person look younger or overly cute

Do not change the gender of the person in the input photo

Do not generate new people (opposite/same sex)

Hairstyle:

Maintain the original hairstyle

Do not change the length, parting, or curl

Only render it in a naturally slightly messy, rough state

Shooting Tone & Color (Reference Reflection):

Express the colors identically to the attached reference photo

Background:

Japanese wooden Ryokan.

Minimize background details

Maintain a dark and blurry state

Clothing Rules (Based on Input Photo):

If the input photo is female → Black {argument name="female attire" default="Yukata"} with red floral patterns in all frames

If the input photo is male → Black silk dress shirt with a {argument name="male attire" default="red tie"} in all frames

1st Frame:

Sad expression like a protagonist in a sad movie

Finger near the mouth

2nd Frame:

Head slightly turned to the side

Show the beautiful profile

Casual selfie feel, no excessive posing

3rd Frame:

Warm and beautiful smile

Hands clasped together, resting the chin on them

Emotion is calm and dreamy atmosphere

Important Additional Instructions:

Use only the one person in the input photo

Do not change gender, face, or atmosphere

Style changes should only be applied to lighting, color, noise, and effects

Use the same face consistently across all frames

Absolute rules regarding effects:

Apply the same cinesteel 800T film style and cinematic light effects in all frames.

Absolute rules regarding effects:

Apply the same red lighting effect on one side and a tasteful motion blur effect from left to right in all frames.
```

## Luxury Closet Mirror Selfie with Dog and Reference Face

Create a 4:5 hyper-realistic 4K mirror-selfie image of a young adult woman kneeling on a light wood floor in a luxury walk-in closet. She wears a fitted black spaghetti-strap mini dress with a square neckline, short hem, and sleek silhouette. Her brown hair with burgundy front highlights is pulled into a low bun with loose strands framing her face. She makes a playful pout, looking directly at her mirror reflection while holding a smartphone with a tan/beige case in her right hand; a small Polaroid photo of Kung-fu Panda is tucked into the back of the case. Accessories include small gold hoop earrings, a thin silver bracelet on her left wrist, rings on her fingers, and a white polished manicure. A small fluffy dog with a merle coat (brown, tan, white patches) and a red/pink collar stands beside her, facing left in profile. The environment is a bright, tidy dressing room with a large floor-length mirror, open wardrobe shelves with folded clothes, hanging garments, white shelving, and visible Sephora and Mango shopping bags. Bright indoor artificial lighting with soft diffusion, eye-level mirror composition, neutral beige/wood/white palette with high-contrast black subject, sharp focus, photorealistic textures. Keep the face hundred percent the same from the reference picture.
```

White plush outerwear + light pink off-shoulder camisole; sweet, cool, and soft. Four poses: holding face with a cold, slightly wronged expression; touching lips calmly, slightly flirtatious; looking away blankly by the table edge; restrained smile with chin resting on hand. High-key soft light, low contrast, shallow shadows; cool white main tone with pink accents; weak background blur, minimal texture; realistic Korean magazine girl group texture, light glass sheen.
```

## Korean Girl Group Style Photo Shoot Prompt

> Minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
I. Composition
1 Layout: Overall 3:4 ratio, four-panel collage, 3:4 small images arranged in 2x2
2 Framing: Close-up to extreme close-up, subject occupies a large proportion of the screen
3 Angle: Eye-level close-up, slight left/right variation, uniform clean background

II. Main Subject
1 Reference Source: Use uploaded reference image as the strict identity source
2 Consistency Requirement: Zero deviation
3 Face: Delicate face shape, slightly large eyes, a small mole below the right eye, long and thin nose bridge
4 Skin Quality: Fair with light pink undertones, delicate skin, slight soft focus
5 Details: Prominent highlights on the nose bridge and mid-face, slight redness on cheeks and nose tip, scattered light freckles

III. Makeup Details
1 Base Makeup: Transparent and clean, light matte finish with slight sheen
2 Blush: Pink concentrated on the cheeks and sides of the nose bridge
3 Eye Makeup: Naturally enlarged, long lashes, clear eyes
4 Lip Makeup: Hydrated, plump lips, gradient from light pink to peach, soft cupid's bow

IV. Hairstyle and Accessories
1 Hairstyle: Black long straight hair, center part, full volume, naturally draped
2 Flyaways: Small amount of hair naturally floating around the forehead
3 Hair Accessories: One pink hair clip on each side, bright pink and light pink colors, metal clip visible

V. Hands and Accessories
1 Nails: Extra-long shape, translucent or light colors, with fine glitter and 3D decorations
2 Rings: Multiple pink cute-shaped rings, candy and floral aesthetics, exaggerated but soft and cute volume
3 Hand Gesture Style: Near the face and lips, highlighting nail and ring details

VI. Clothing and Material
1 Top: Fuzzy textured jacket or sleeves, white fluffy and soft
2 Inner Wear: Light pink camisole or top, off-shoulder or one-shoulder effect
3 Overall Style: Sweet and soft, with a touch of cool edge

VII. Four Panel Actions and Expressions
1 Top Left: Hands cupping face close to the camera, expression is cold and slightly wronged, looking directly at the camera
2 Top Right: Finger near the lips or lightly touching the lip edge, eyes calm and slightly teasing, closer extreme close-up
3 Bottom Left: Chin or arm resting on the edge of a table, eyes looking sideways, expression is dazed and vacant
4 Bottom Right: Hand clenched lightly against the chin, smiling but restrained, looking directly at the camera, more approachable demeanor

VIII. Lighting and Tone
1 Lighting: High-key soft light, very shallow shadows, uniform overall
2 Color Tone: Predominantly cool white background, pink accents highlighted, skin tone leans cool pink
3 Contrast: Low contrast, clean and transparent, not heavy

IX. Texture and Visual Effect
1 Clarity: Features and hair strands are clear, skin details preserved but with slight blurring/softening
2 Depth of Field: Background blur is very weak or almost textureless, subject is more prominent
3 Atmosphere: Sweet Korean girl group magazine photo style, sweet and cute, delicate and dreamy, slight glass-like sheen.
```

GLOBAL STYLE & QUALITY

Ultra-high resolution hyper-realistic photography, natural skin texture, real-world lighting physics, true-to-life proportions, cinematic shallow depth of field, premium lifestyle photo realism, no stylization, no CGI look, no illustration artifacts

FACE

Soft youthful face with natural symmetry, smooth realistic skin pores, subtle blush on cheeks, gentle smile
One eye playfully winking, the other eye open and expressive
Natural makeup, light peach lips, soft eyeliner, no exaggerated beauty edits
BODY
Slim feminine body with soft natural curves, realistic waist-to-hip ratio
Relaxed but confident posture, shoulders slightly back
Body language is flirty yet casual, not posed like a model, feels spontaneous

SKIN

Fair skin tone with very subtle sun warmth, smooth transitions
No artificial shine, no plastic skin
Natural light reflecting softly on shoulders and arms
HAIR
Long brown hair tied in a high ponytail, loose natural waves
A few soft strands framing the face
Hair texture is realistic, individual strands visible under sunlight

CAMERA & ANGLE

Medium full-body shot
Camera positioned slightly below chest level, angled to the side
Subject turned sideways, body angled while face looks toward camera
Natural lens compression, realistic focal length (50–85mm look)
SETTING
Outdoor rooftop or terrace environment
{argument name="lighting time" default="Golden hour sunset"} lighting
Background softly blurred with natural bokeh
 No identifiable people in background, only vague architectural shapes

SCENE SETUP

Casual candid moment
Subject holding a smartphone in one hand
Other hand raised near face making a peace sign (V sign)
Feels like a spontaneous lifestyle photo, not staged
BIKINI / OUTFIT
Top: Strapless {argument name="bikini color" default="grey"} tube bikini-style top, tight fit, exposing shoulders and collarbones
Bottom: High-waisted denim short shorts with lace-up front detail, distressed edges
Outfit is minimal, youthful, summer casual
No logos, no distracting patterns

PROPS (FLATLAY FEEL)

Smartphone held naturally in hand
No additional accessories
Focus stays on body language and outfit
EXPRESSION & MOOD
Playful, confident, teasing expression
Winking adds flirt energy
Mood is light, warm, approachable, seductive without being explicit
ATMOSPHERE

Warm sunset tones
Soft highlights on skin
Balanced exposure — no overblown whites, no crushed shadows
Natural ambient light only

FINAL RESULT GOAL
A hyper-realistic seductive lifestyle photograph that looks like it was taken by a professional photographer during golden hour,
natural, believable, premium social media aesthetic,
 bold but classy, flirty without exaggeration,
indistinguishable from a real photo.
```

## Abstract Chaotic Scribble Art Prompt

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Convert the uploaded face photo into a very abstract and chaotic scribble art with very loose, scattered and extremely random black ink lines, creating an image of the person in the photo standing in a good pose, wearing a proper shirt and pants. The lines should barely make out the face, emphasizing chaos and abstraction rather than clear definition. Maintain the original expression and proportions as a general underlying composition.
```

## Nine-Panel Face Consistency Grid Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the provided reference image as the sole identity and facial structure reference. Create a 3 by 3 grid of face focused portraits of the same woman, maintaining identical facial features, proportions, expression baseline, and realism across all frames.

Each panel should show a distinct head angle and facial perspective, while preserving lighting consistency, lens realism, and natural skin texture. No change in hairstyle, makeup style, or facial identity.

Grid breakdown

Top row
1.Front facing neutral angle, eyes looking directly into camera
2.Three quarter view facing right, slight head tilt, eyes forward
3.Full right side profile, clean silhouette of nose, lips, and jawline

Middle row
4. Three quarter view facing left, soft head tilt, confident gaze
5. Slight low angle looking down toward the camera, subtle power pose
6. Slight high angle looking upward, relaxed expression

Bottom row
7. Head turned right with eyes looking back toward camera
8. Head turned left with eyes looking back toward camera
9. Near rear three quarter angle showing cheekbone, jawline, and ear contour

Technical and visual requirements

Ultra realistic portrait photography
85mm lens look, shallow depth of field
Sharp facial detail, visible skin texture, natural pores
Soft daylight illumination with gentle shadow falloff
Neutral background with no distractions
Consistent framing so all faces align cleanly within the grid
High resolution, editorial quality, fashion and beauty standard

Strict constraints

Do not alter facial structure
Do not stylize or cartoonize
Do not change expression drastically
No text, no watermark, no distortion
Same person in all nine frames
```

## Ultra-realistic café lifestyle portrait with identity lock prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an ultra-realistic, high-end lifestyle portrait of one adult subject, captured in a calm, elegant, modern café environment. The subject is seated casually inside a circular wooden frame embedded with warm LED rim lighting, surrounded by lush green foliage and soft floral decor. The pose is relaxed and natural: one leg crossed over the other, body slightly angled, shoulders relaxed, hands resting naturally while holding a small bouquet of fresh flowers. Overall mood is warm, clean, elegant, youthful, and approachable — pure realism only. No stylization, no fantasy, no cinematic exaggeration.

Use my uploaded face image as the ONLY facial identity reference

Maintain 100% facial accuracy: exact bone structure, eye shape, eyelid fold, eyebrow shape, nose bridge and tip, lip shape, jawline, chin, cheek volume, hairline, and facial proportions

Identity must remain perfectly consistent from all angles (front, 3/4, side, seated pose, slight head tilt)

No face blending, no beautification, no symmetry correction

No AI face drift

Preserve real skin texture: pores, fine lines, natural imperfections

Expression: soft, natural smile, relaxed and genuine

Soft pastel long-sleeve knit top (dusty pink / muted rose tone)

High-waisted, straight-fit beige or cream trousers

Clean white chunky sneakers

Minimal accessories only (simple earrings, smartwatch or minimal wrist accessory)

Hair neatly styled, natural, pulled back or softly tied

Natural makeup: realistic skin tones, no heavy retouching

Modern café or lifestyle studio interior

Circular wooden structure with embedded warm LED light ring

Green plants and floral arrangements integrated into the frame

Soft bokeh background with warm ambient lighting

Clean, uncluttered setting with depth and realism

Full-body or 3/4 body vertical portrait

Eye-level camera angle

50mm–85mm lens look

Natural perspective, no distortion

Subject centered within the circular frame

Shallow depth of field, background softly blurred

Soft, warm, diffused lighting

LED rim light gently outlining the circular frame

Even facial illumination with natural shadow falloff

No harsh highlights, no blown whites

Realistic skin reflections and depth

Negative prompt:

No stylization

No cinematic color grading

No beauty filters

No smooth or plastic skin

No exaggerated proportions

No painterly or illustrated look

No artificial glow

No face morphing or identity shift

Ultra-photorealistic

Editorial lifestyle photography quality

Natural colors, true-to-life skin tones

Identity remains unchanged and accurate from every angle
```

## Sydney Sweeney Vintage Film Portrait Prompt

> Sinematik, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded reference image as the absolute identity anchor. Preserve the subject’s facial features, facial proportions, bone structure, eyes, nose, lips, skin tone, hairline, hair texture, body shape, and overall likeness with maximum fidelity. Do not beautify, idealize, or alter the person in any way. A cinematic, vintage-style outdoor portrait of the same person. For each generation, vary the subject’s pose naturally (e.g., slight body angle changes, subtle lean, head tilt, arm positioning) while keeping the same prop: a bright {argument name="prop color" default="red"} lollipop held near the lips or face. Maintain a calm, confident expression. The subject wears a white strapless bandeau top and high-waisted light-blue distressed denim shorts. Lighting is soft, warm, and natural during golden hour. The image has a strong analog film aesthetic with heavy grain, dust, scratches, subtle fading, and a warm brown-orange color cast, resembling an old 35mm photograph. Background is softly blurred with shallow depth of field. Framing remains cinematic and consistent.",
  "identity_preservation": {
    "face_likeness": "maximum",
    "body_proportions": "exact_match",
    "hair": "match_reference"
  },
  "pose_variation": {
    "enabled": true,
    "mode": "random_per_generation",
    "constraints": [
      "natural human poses only",
      "no extreme or distorted angles",
      "no change to body shape or identity"
    ]
  },
  "prop": {
    "name": "{argument name="prop name" default="red lollipop"}",
    "consistency": "same_prop_every_generation"
  },
  "style_keywords": [
    "vintage film photography",
    "analog grain",
    "cinematic portrait",
    "retro aesthetic",
    "natural light",
    "shallow depth of field",
    "realistic skin texture"
  ],
  "camera_settings": {
    "lens": "35mm",
    "aperture": "f/1.8",
    "iso": 800,
    "film_grain": "heavy",
    "vignette": "light"
  },
  "negative_prompt": [
    "face change",
    "different person",
    "altered identity",
    "idealized beauty",
    "smooth plastic skin",
    "CGI",
    "illustration",
    "digital HDR look",
    "modern studio lighting",
    "missing prop",
    "changed prop"
  ],
  "generation_settings": {
    "image_weight": "1.5–2.0",
    "face_consistency": "max",
    "denoising_strength": "0.2–0.35",
    "cfg_scale": "low_to_medium",
    "aspect_ratio": "16:9"
  }
}

## Hyper-Realistic Gym Mirror Selfie Prompt (iPhone 17 Pro Simulation)

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 9:16 smartphone aspect ratio). A raw, high-contrast [Gym Interior Mirror Capture] taken with an iPhone 17 Pro using the 0.5x ultra-wide-angle lens. NO ARTIFICIAL BOKEH. Every focal plane remains SHARP and in focus, from the subject's skin to the distant gym equipment.
Subject & Identity Locking:
•Identity: Strictly preserve the exact heart-shaped facial structure and unique features of the woman in Image 1 (Selfie).
•Bio-Fidelity: Render high-fidelity "TrueLens" biological physics: visible micro-pores, a natural "satin-finish" night hydration glow, and fine vellus hair catching the overhead light ONLY along the jawline.
•Silhouette Calibration (MAXIMUM UFF-FACTOR): Prioritize a dominant structural upper-torso volume and substantial, thick-toned anatomical depth in the lower extremities. Focus on an aggressive architectural tapering at the midsection to maximize the S-curve geometry.
Outfit & Textural Interaction:
•Clothing: A high-density, matte-black technical textile tee and matching high-tension minimalist athletic shorts.
•Tension: The technical fabric exhibits extreme mechanical tension and a vacuum-tight fit, creating realistic textural expansion and "tension lines" where the material contours to her fit silhouette.
•Matter Physics: White high-density cotton crew socks showing realistic textural contact and weight distribution against the tiled floor.
Pose & Composition:
•Pose: A 3/4 side-profile mirror selfie. She is standing with an axial rotation of the torso toward the mirror, arching her spine and popping her hip to highlight the symmetrical 3D volume of her physique.
•Action: Her RIGHT HAND holds a smartphone with a decorative casing, capturing the full-body "fit-check" reflection.
•UI Overlay: A white digital clock timestamp in the top-left corner reads "8:33 PM" in a simple sans-serif font.
Environment & Social Realism:
•Lighting: Direct, harsh overhead industrial lighting creating high-contrast specular highlights and deep, defining shadows that amplify her 3D form.
•Background (SHARP): A detailed commercial gym interior. A vibrant neon-green structural pillar, a white tiled floor with visible grout lines, and dark athletic benches are all perfectly IN FOCUS. No artificial blur.
Quality: Raw unedited social-media dump aesthetic, high dynamic range, visible digital grain in the deep shadows (ISO 1600 style), 8k resolution. Everything is sharp, detailed, and physically real.
```

## Low-Light Phone Selfie with Screen Glow

> Minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Vertical, realistic low-light phone selfie in a dim bedroom at night. Same face as reference (no changes). Cool bluish-purple screen glow lighting on a pale face; background mostly dark with beige blackout curtains on the left. Sleepy, cozy, late-night lo-fi vibe.
Close-up, slightly low angle. Subject lying on stomach, propped on a grey pillow with subtle pattern. Messy black bedhead hair with wispy bangs. Soft, dreamy/blank stare, mouth slightly open. Hand near face, index finger touching lowerlip.
Wearing a {argument name="clothing item" default="black sleeveless camisole"}; thin lace strap visible, left shoulder bare. Soft focus, visible grain/noise, high contrast between lit face and dark background.
Negative Prompt:
bright daylight, sunshine, studio/flash lighting, warm/orange tones, outdoor, sharp focus, professional camera, 4k clean, smooth skin, heavy makeup, cartoon, anime, 3D render, deformed hands, extra/missing fingers, out-of-focus face.
```

## Terminator Girl

```
use [uploaded image] as Woman Refference, A wounded female Terminator robot from a science fiction film, an Asian woman with long hair, a beautiful half-face, a cold demeanor, tanned skin, her left cheek injured and the skin peeling off to reveal the mechanical components underneath, a mechanical eyeball and a steel skeleton for her face. The film is futuristic, absurd, and imaginative, featuring a thick, tattered red leather jacket, battle marks, a post-apocalyptic atmosphere, dim lighting, and a faulty, leaky electrical environment. A masterpiece of science fiction, the film boasts a stunningly realistic post-apocalyptic aesthetic, rendered in ultra-high definition.

## Overhead Smartphone Selfie with Black Cat

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "intent": {
    "image_type": "selfie",
    "era_preset": "modern_standard",
    "notes": "Maximizing physical realism by defining light-material interaction and sensor failure modes."
  },
  "subjects": {
    "count": 1,
    "primary": {
      "type": "person",
      "age_range": "20-25",
      "gender_presentation": "female",
      "region_target": "unspecified",
      "expression": "neutral, looking up",
      "pose": "looking directly into camera from below",
      "gaze": "looking into lens"
    },
    "additional": [
      {
        "type": "black cat",
        "position": "floor level, looking up",
        "material": "dense organic fur with translucent whiskers"
      }
    ]
  },
  "framing_and_perspective": {
    "framing": "high_angle_plunging",
    "subject_fill_ratio": 0.82,
    "camera_angle": "directly_overhead_downward",
    "distance": "arm_length",
    "camera_class": "smartphone_front",
    "lens_equiv_mm": 24,
    "distortion": {
      "type": "mild_barrel",
      "strength_1to5": 4
    },
    "perspective_effects": {
      "foreground_scale_emphasis_1to5": 5,
      "nose_emphasis_1to5": 3,
      "ear_recession_1to5": 4
    },
    "depth_of_field": {
      "mode": "minimal",
      "strength_1to5": 2,
      "focus_target": "eyes"
    }
  },
  "lighting_and_exposure": {
    "key_light": {
      "type": "overhead_panel",
      "direction": "top_down",
      "hardness_1to5": 5,
      "halo_bloom_1to5": 2
    },
    "exposure_behavior": {
      "auto_exposure": true,
      "hdr_tone_mapping_1to5": 4,
      "overall_brightness_1to5": 5,
      "contrast_1to5": 3,
      "shadow_depth_1to5": 2,
      "highlight_overflow_1to5": 5
    },
    "failure_modes_active": {
      "highlight_clipping": true,
      "shadow_chroma_noise": true,
      "denoise_smear": false,
      "sharpen_halos": true,
      "motion_blur": false,
      "jpeg_ringing_banding": false,
      "banding_in_gradients": false,
      "focus_falloff_edge_softness": true
    }

  "computational_pipeline": {

      "method": "edge_aware",
      "strength_1to5": 2,
      "specular_preservation": "required"
    },
    "sharpening": {
      "strength_1to5": 3,
      "halo_risk_1to5": 2
    },
    "noise_and_denoise": {
      "shadow_chroma_noise_1to5": 3,
      "denoise_smear_1to5": 2,
      "grain_strength_1to5": 2
    },
    "compression": {
      "jpeg_artifacts_1to5": 1,
      "banding_risk_1to5": 1
    },
    "white_balance": {
      "mode": "auto",
      "highlight_temp_bias": "cool",
      "shadow_temp_bias": "neutral"
    }
  },
  "region_and_face_feature_sliders": {
    "non_stereotype_policy": "sliders_over_labels",
    "beauty_mark_under_right_eye": false,
    "sliders_1to5": {

      "eye_emphasis_1to5": 4,

## 2x2 Grid Collage of Woman in Kitchen

> Fotogerçekçi görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic photorealistic 2×2 grid collage image, adult woman (21+), same person repeated in all frames with locked facial identity and body proportions. Slim athletic body with curvy hips, narrow defined waist, flat toned abdomen, smooth thighs. Fair to light beige skin with warm neutral undertones, natural smooth texture with slight realistic sheen.
Soft heart-shaped to oval face, full cheeks with gentle blush, smooth feminine jawline, rounded chin. Medium almond-shaped light brown to hazel eyes with playful, happy expression. Medium-thickness softly arched dark-blonde eyebrows. Small-to-medium straight nose with soft rounded tip. Medium-full lips, natural soft pink color, slight gloss, wide cheerful smile showing teeth.
Long blonde hair with warm golden highlights, styled in a high half-ponytail, straight texture with light natural volume, loose face-framing strands.
Wearing a fitted white crop top with front chest cut-out, asymmetrical off-shoulder design, long sleeves featuring criss-cross lace-up cutouts along the arms, partially open back. Paired with high-waisted pastel {argument name="shorts color" default="light-green"} fitted shorts, smooth stretchy fabric, snug body-hugging fit.
Grid composition: one single image divided into four equal panels (2×2) with thin white borders. Each panel shows a different pose of the same woman:
• leaning forward against the kitchen counter, playful smile
• standing sideways with hips angled, cheerful expression
• over-the-shoulder pose showing back and hips
• leaning back with one hand on waist, confident smile
Environment consistent across all panels: modern indoor kitchen, light beige polished marble countertop, white vertically paneled cabinets with matte finish, neutral wall tones, small black and stainless-steel kitchen appliances visible.
Vertical 9:16 final aspect ratio, full collage framed vertically, subject centered in each panel, balanced spacing, natural smartphone composition.
Soft indoor overhead kitchen lighting, even illumination, minimal diffused shadows, gentle highlights on hair and skin. Neutral color grading with slight warmth, medium contrast, natural saturation.
Shot on smartphone wide-angle camera, 24mm equivalent, eye-level to slightly high angle, sharp focus, minimal distortion.
Extremely high realism, accurate anatomy, consistent face across all four panels, no blur, no artifacts.
```

Her hair appears healthy, dark brown, and waist-length, with a few strands of hair blowing across her face and some small wisps fluttering in the wind.

Her lips are coated with a gradient pink lip gloss, giving a full and moisturized look. Her skin is radiant and dewy, with light reflecting off her face and body, creating a luminous effect.
She has an ideal hourglass figure and full bust.

Pose: She is standing, a traditional black bag hanging from her belt, with a sweet smile and looking at the camera as if taking a selfie herself.

Clothing: She is wearing traditionally inspired clothing: a cream-colored dress with a delicate texture, paired with a black embroidered vest adorned with floral metal embellishments, and a wide gold belt with circular details. She is also wearing gold jewelry, including a necklace and earrings that softly reflect the sunlight.

Atmosphere: Natural outdoor environment
Stone pathway in the foreground
Green grass and trees in the background
Open sky with soft clouds
Realistic environment with clear depth and detail
No artificial blur or bokeh.

Color palette: Warm neutral colors, soft greens, gold accents
Tone: Natural and warm sunlight
Contrast: Balanced, realistic
Highlights: Soft sunlight lighting on the face, especially the eyes and jewelry from front.
Mood: Elegant, serene, natural
Image size 2:3, camera angle: Full body shot, high angle.
Do not alter her face from the image 100%.

## Ultra-Realistic Bikini Collage in Outdoor Shower

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic photorealistic image, adult woman (21+), curvy yet toned feminine body, light to medium warm beige skin tone with soft peach undertones, natural skin texture with visible pores, slightly wet skin with subtle reflective sheen.
Soft oval face shape, gently rounded cheeks with natural blush, smooth feminine jawline, rounded chin. Almond-shaped medium brown eyes with calm, confident expression, slightly hooded eyelids, natural lashes. Medium-thickness softly arched dark brown eyebrows. Medium straight nose with soft rounded tip. Full natural lips, pink with warm undertone, lightly glossy, relaxed closed lips with faint confident smile.
Dark brown hair, slicked back wet look, center-parted, smooth strands pulled back behind shoulders.
Wearing a deep royal blue textured bikini, triangle bikini top with thin neck-tie straps, supportive natural fit, matching low-rise bikini bottoms, fabric slightly shimmering when wet.
Scene set in a private outdoor stone shower courtyard. Large gray stone slab walls with visible cracks, weathering, damp stains. Wet stone tile floor with reflections. A metal wall-mounted shower spout releasing a thin stream of water, droplets frozen mid-air. Potted green plant in a brown clay pot near a simple wooden spa bench.
2x2 grid collage layout, four different poses of the same woman:
• kneeling pose with one leg forward, one arm raised behind head
• close-up selfie angle focusing on face and upper torso
• kneeling upright pose with direct eye contact
• seated pose with legs extended diagonally, one arm supporting body
Natural daylight from open sky, soft diffused lighting, gentle shadows, subtle highlights on wet skin and stone surfaces. Neutral color grading with slight warmth, medium contrast, natural saturation.
Shot on wide-angle smartphone camera, 24mm equivalent, sharp focus, slight wide-angle edge distortion, realistic handheld perspective.
Extremely high realism, face consistency across all frames, accurate anatomy, no blur, no distortion, no AI artifacts.
Aspect ratio 9:16
```

## Photorealistic Gym Selfie Portrait with Specific Composition and Color Palette

> Fotogerçekçi moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A young woman with a slender figure and graceful curves, with wavy dark long hair, is taking a mirror selfie in front of a mirror in a modern gym. She is wearing a set of eye-catching rose pink sportswear, including a tight sports bra and tight sports shorts, showcasing an excellent waist-to-hip ratio and fair skin tone. In terms of composition, she is standing sideways within the frame of a black Smith machine, holding her phone up with her right hand, stretching her left hand up to grasp the top beam of the equipment, and lightly lifting her left leg, resting the line of her leg on the horizontal barbell. The environmental lighting is sufficient and uniform, presenting a cool industrial feel, with the surfaces of the metal equipment reflecting cold highlights, and the floor being a dark gray non-slip rubber mat with fine particles. In the background, clearly visible are arranged barbell plates, orange fitness equipment supports, and a white wall with black artistic lettering. The color scheme features a strong contrast between high-saturation rose pink and calm black and gray, supplemented by a few touches of orange. The overall image quality is delicate, with distinct light and shadow layers, presenting a modern, fashionable, and vibrant fitness atmosphere.
```

{

Create a **cosmic galaxy watercolor portrait** where the face remains soft and realistic but is gently merged with nebula textures. Hair follows the exact reference style, with subtle stardust highlights woven through the strands. Clothing: simple dark or neutral attire that fades into swirling galaxy patterns. Background: deep space filled with vibrant nebulas, glowing stars, floating light particles, and watercolor galaxies in shades of indigo, teal, violet, and cosmic blue. Soft ethereal rim light outlines the silhouette. Fine pencil sketch lines mixed with watercolor bleeds, smooth gradients, dreamy depth, cinematic and artistic masterpiece quality.",

}

## Ultra-Wide Candid Mirror Documentation Portrait Prompt

> Minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 9:16 smartphone aspect ratio). A raw, high-contrast [Candid Mirror Documentation] captured with an iPhone 17 Pro using the 0.5x ultra-wide-angle optic. NO ARTIFICIAL BOKEH. Everything remains SHARP and in focus from the subject's skin to the distant urban masonry.
Subject & Identity Locking:
•Identity: Strictly preserve the exact facial structure and unique features of the woman in Image 1 (Selfie).
•Appearance: Short dark auburn-red hair styled in a sleek, chin-length architectural bob.
•Bio-Fidelity: Render high-fidelity "TrueLens" biological physics: visible micro-pores, a natural "satin-finish" skin hydration glow, and fine vellus hair catching the light ONLY along the jawline. Her skin features a healthy, uniform tone.
•Silhouette Calibration (MAXIMUM UFF-FACTOR): Prioritize a dominant structural upper-torso volume and substantial, thick-toned anatomical depth in the lower extremities. Emphasize an aggressive architectural tapering at the midsection to maximize the S-curve geometry.
Outfit & Textural Interaction:
•Clothing: A high-density ribbed light-pink textile bodice featuring delicate ivory-colored architectural lace trim. Paired with high-tension minimalist elasticated lower apparel.
•Tension: The technical fabric exhibits extreme mechanical tension and a vacuum-tight fit, creating realistic textural expansion, skin-to-textile indentations, and "compression lines" where the material contours to her fit silhouette.
•Action: The subject is captured performing a manual upward adjustment of the bodice hem, creating realistic fabric-stress vectors and highlighting the fit anatomical depth of the midriff.
Pose & Composition:
•Pose: A straight-on frontal composition within a large circular reflective frame. She is standing with a bilateral hip-pop to accentuate the symmetrical volume and fit proportions of her physique.
•Action: Her RIGHT HAND holds a white smartphone with a clear protective casing, capturing the full-body "fit-check" reflection.
Environment & Social Realism:
•Lighting: Naturalistic indoor daylight streaming from a side-view window, creating high-contrast specular highlights and deep, defining shadows that amplify her 3D form.
•Background (SHARP): A detailed domestic interior showing uncurated realism. A bed with textured grey compressed linens, a digital musical synthesizer, and a dark suitcase are all perfectly IN FOCUS. Visible through the glass is a sharp, red-brick urban structure. No artificial blur.
Quality: Raw unedited social-media dump aesthetic, high dynamic range, visible digital grain in the deep shadows, 8k resolution. Everything is sharp, detailed, and physically real.
```

## Selfie Portrait with Cold/Warm Lighting Contrast

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Theme] A young woman with dark brown/black hair styled in a bun, with a few wispy bangs falling on her forehead; looking directly at the camera, with a calm and slightly languid expression. Right hand supporting her cheek, left hand holding a phone in a selfie pose.

[Makeup and Skin Texture] Natural, clear makeup, clean eye makeup, distinct eyelashes, moist lips; extremely delicate skin, the overall complexion shows a blue-purple sheen under the cold light.

[Clothing] White thin-strap low-cut top (simple, fitted style).

[Accessories] Layered transparent pink beaded bracelet and a thin gold chain bracelet (with small flower/clover pendant) on the wrist; nails painted with light nude nail polish.

[Props] A bright yellow phone/phone case is very eye-catching (located in the lower left corner of the frame), enhancing the realism of a “casual social selfie.”

[Scene] Dim restaurant/bar atmosphere, with thick black curtains as the background; white round table with wine glasses and small objects, multiple warm yellow table lamps creating a cozy ambient light.

[Light and Color] The subject is illuminated by cold blue-purple fill light (such as screen light/LED fill light), while the background emits warm yellow tungsten light, forming a sharp cold/warm contrast; the background is slightly blurred to highlight the portrait.

[Photography Language] Vertical composition, close-up half-body shot (above the chest), slightly high-angle/eye-level perspective, with the texture of a phone portrait mode, medium-shallow depth of field, and the image texture is “clear yet soft.”
```

## Atmospheric Indoor Mirror Selfie with 'Pure and Desire' Aesthetic

> Minimal yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Overview: This photo captures a highly atmospheric indoor Mirror Selfie. It freezes a moment of a young Asian woman in a relaxed state, featuring a strong "Pure and Desire" style (Innocent yet alluring) and an authentic social media lifestyle vibe.  Subject Features: Female, with fair complexion (cool porcelain skin). Her long black hair is tied back (into a low ponytail or bun), with air bangs framing her forehead and face. The makeup is exquisite yet natural, emphasizing reddish lips and a clear, translucent foundation.  Expression & Mood: Her eyes are either dreamy or focused on the phone screen, with her head tilted slightly, conveying a sense of nonchalant elegance. Her mouth is closed, and her expression is calm with a hint of sultriness.  Pose: Seated. She sits on a black leather chair, leaning back. Her right hand rests lazily behind her head/neck area, while her left hand holds the phone (silver iPhone) to take the mirror selfie. This pose naturally showcases her neck and shoulder lines.  Outfit Details: The core visual point is the "slouchy / effortlessly disheveled" style. She wears a white deep V-neck long-sleeved top (the material looks soft, like modal or fine knit). The neckline slips off, revealing her collarbones, a large area of skin on her chest, and visible black thin bra straps, creating a classic "black and white contrast" and a hint of sexiness. She wears black trousers on the bottom.  Environment & Lighting: The background is very simple, featuring beige/grey wallpaper and wooden door frames or mirror frames on the sides. The lighting is soft and even, resembling indoor natural light or softbox lighting, giving the skin a delicate texture. There is a black hair tie on her wrist (adding a touch of realistic detail).  Vibe: Intimate, casual, the refined daily life of an urban woman, and a sense of relaxation.
```

       "background_elements": [         "Large white textured pillows (tufted or knit)",         "White sheets",         "Dark wall"       ],       "ambient_lighting": "Purple LED strip light running horizontally behind the headboard",       "atmosphere": "Dimly lit room with colored accent lighting"     },     "style_and_technical": {       "aesthetic": [         "E-girl",         "Y2K grunge",         "2000s digital aesthetic"       ],       "lighting_technique": "Direct on-camera flash, harsh high-contrast lighting on subject against darker background",       "camera_settings": {         "angle": "High-angle wide selfie",         "distortion": "Slight wide-angle distortion",         "color_profile": "Full color, natural color rendering with vibrant neon purple accent"       },       "aspect_ratio": "3:4"      }    }  }

Motion control prompt:
Generate a realistic video using the attached reference image as the identity anchor. Preserve the subject’s overall appearance exactly as shown in the reference image, including face structure, skin tone, hair color and texture, body proportions, and general silhouette. Maintain strong identity consistency across all frames while allowing natural motion, subtle expression changes, and realistic body movement. Do not alter the subject’s physical traits; only introduce smooth, lifelike animation and camera motion. Ensure lighting, realism, and visual fidelity remain consistent with the reference image throughout the video.

## 3x3 Cinematic Profile Grid with Identity Lock

> Sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a 3×3 grid of cinematic portraits using the uploaded image as the exact face reference (same identity, same facial features, no changes).

Busy downtown Chicago street background, real urban life, traffic bokeh, pedestrians, depth and motion.

Wardrobe: hip-hop streetwear — torn denim jeans, crop tops / hoodies, baseball cap, layered chains, hoop earrings, boots. Outfit variations but same vibe.

Each image uses a different camera angle & pose:
• Front-facing portrait
• Low-angle power shot
• Side profile
• Over-the-shoulder
• Full-body walking shot
• Crouched street pose
• High-angle candid
• Three-quarter portrait
• Back view with head turn
Style: cinematic realism, shallow depth of field, natural city lighting, subtle film grain, fashion-editorial look, consistent face across all 9 images.
❌ NEGATIVE PROMPT
Different faces, face distortion, AI drift, cartoon, CGI, plastic skin, text, watermark
🧠 Pro tip:
Upload your face first → enable face reference / identity lock → generate in square format for a perfect profile grid.
```

## Candid Night Car Selfie with Flash

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Scene: Nighttime inside a car (driver’s seat, on the left side of the frame). The instrument cluster and center console glow with crisp cool white/blue backlighting. Outside the window is a dim roadway with streetlight bokeh, adding a slight sense of motion and an urban night atmosphere.  Subject & emotion: A young adult woman sits on the left side of the image and turns her head to the right to look back at the camera. She makes direct eye contact with a playful, subtly flirty “caught candid” vibe. She holds her left hand horizontally across the lower half of her face, revealing only her eyes and brow area—an instantly memorable gesture.  Hair & texture: Long, straight hair with a slightly center part; light blonde with even lighter highlighted streaks. The strands look smooth and glossy, catching reflections from the flash.  Outfit: A black spaghetti-strap bra-style top paired with low-rise jeans, giving an authentic “night out / getting into the car after a party” social-media feel.  Detail boosters: Nude/pale pink manicure; a delicate chain bracelet on the wrist. Warm fair skin tone. The direct flash creates high contrast and soft shadows that sculpt the body and add dimension.  Photography language: Clear smartphone on-camera flash in a dark environment. Vertical close medium framing, shot from the passenger side/back-seat angle, capturing her upper body along with the steering wheel, door trim, and dashboard—very Instagram/social-media candid.
```

## Ultra-Photorealistic Candid Smartphone Selfie Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-photorealistic smartphone selfie photo (front camera), portrait 4:5. A young adult woman seated at a kitchen table, centered framing from mid-torso to top of head. She has shoulder-length dark brown hair with soft layers, curtain bangs, and two small black hair clips on both sides. Expression is calm and slightly pouty, natural skin texture, minimal makeup. She raises her hand close to her face making a peace/V sign, with the fingers framing one eye. She wears a white ribbed camisole with delicate lace trim.
Lighting is strong direct morning sunlight coming from a large window on the left, creating hard shadows and bright highlights across her face, chest, and the table.
Environment: a bright modern kitchen with white walls and cabinets; a refrigerator in the background covered with small colorful magnets/souvenir magnets.
Foreground on the table: a white pump lotion bottle, a blue plastic water bottle, an amber pill/vitamin bottle, a pack of wet wipes, and some snack packaging including a red rectangular box, all slightly cluttered and casually placed.
Style: realistic color, natural contrast, sharp focus, high detail, authentic candid selfie look, HDR-like dynamic range, subtle film grain, no retouching, no beauty filter.
Negative Prompt (use this)
Blurry, low-res, over-smoothed skin, heavy makeup, glam retouching, unrealistic doll face, warped hands, extra fingers, missing fingers, deformed peace sign, crossed eyes, bad anatomy, distorted background, duplicated objects, text, watermark, logo, posterized lighting.
Suggested Settings
Aspect ratio: 4:5
Lens look: smartphone wide (24–28mm equivalent)
Quality: high / ultra detail
Style strength: low–medium (keep it natural)
also make watermark "doctorwasif" at bottom right corner
```

## Hyper-Detailed Prone Selfie with Anatomical Realism

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 9:16 smartphone aspect ratio). A raw, high-contrast [Mirror Capture] taken with an iPhone 17 Pro. NO ARTIFICIAL BOKEH. All planes of the image are SHARP and in focus from the subject's feet to the background window blinds.
Subject & Identity Locking:
•Identity: Strictly preserve the exact face and unique features of the woman in Image 1 (Selfie).
•Bio-Fidelity: Render high-fidelity "TrueLens" skin physics: visible micro-pores, natural "satin-finish" night hydration glow, and fine peach fuzz catching the light ONLY along the jawline.
•Expression: Looking into the mirror reflection with a magnetic, "cheeky" gaze, performing a subtle labial compression (lip bite). Her hair falls naturally over her shoulder.
•Silhouette Calibration (MAXIMUM UFF-FACTOR): Replicate the high-impact prone proportions. Focus on the structural lower-torso volume and substantial, thick-toned anatomical depth. Emphasize the aggressive architectural tapering at the midsection and the deep S-curve of the lower back.
Outfit & Material Realism:
•Clothing: A high-tension light-{argument name="top color" default="yellow"} cropped jersey-knit top and ultra-fitted {argument name="shorts color" default="white"} high-elasticity micro-shorts with a wide waistband.
•Tension: The technical fabric shows extreme mechanical tension and a vacuum-tight fit, creating realistic physical interaction and "tension lines" where the material contours to her fit silhouette.
•Matter Physics: Realistic skin-to-surface compression (squish) is visible where her torso and thighs interact with the high-texture grey bedding. Her raised feet show detailed anatomical sole textures.
Pose & Composition:
•Pose: A prone horizontal alignment (lying on her stomach) with an axial rotation of the torso toward the mirror. Her lower limbs are elevated at the knees.
•Action: Her RIGHT HAND holds a pink smartphone at a natural angle, capturing the full-body "fit-check" reflection.
Environment & Social Realism:
•Lighting: Naturalistic, low-light indoor setting. The scene is side-lit, creating high-contrast specular highlights on her skin and the {argument name="top color" default="yellow"} fabric, casting sharp, pitch-black shadows that define her 3D volume.
•Background (SHARP): A detailed domestic bedroom. A window with horizontal blinds, dark heavy curtains, and a grey textured faux-fur duvet are all perfectly IN FOCUS. No artificial depth-of-field.
Quality: Raw unedited social-media dump aesthetic, high dynamic range, visible digital grain in the deep shadows (ISO 1600 style), 8k resolution. Single frame.
```

## Moody Cinematic Studio Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Scene studio = true
studio background color = "{argument name="background color" default="deep_midnight_blue"}"
studio lighting = "soft_directional_spotlight_low_key"
focus subject = "male_same_as_reference"
focus style = "crisp_subject_priority"
depth background = "cinematic_smooth_bokeh"

subject pose = "relaxed_confident_stance"
subject expression = "calm_confident_with_edge"
subject outfit type = "cool_modern_streetwear"
subject outfit details = {
    top: "structured_leather_or_bomber_jacket",
    inner_layer: "dark_fitted_tshirt",
    bottom: "tailored_slim_trousers",
    accessories: "minimal_watch_or_chain",
    overall_vibe: "effortless_cool_high_fashion"
}

camera lens = "85mm_prime_portrait"
camera framing = "mid_torso_shot"
camera mood = "moody_studio_cinematic"
render quality = "ultra_hd_hyper_realistic"
```

## Close-Up Selfie Portrait with Soft Indoor Lighting

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Subject: A young woman with light-colored skin and dark blonde/light brown hair casually tied in a low or mid messy bun, with a few loose strands framing her cheeks. Expression & Mood: Slight head tilt with a soft, playful smile; gentle eye contact directly toward the camera; casual, relaxed “social media snapshot” vibe. Makeup: Very natural light makeup — noticeable lashes, subtle blush, and naturally rosy glossy lips. Outfit: {argument name="top garment" default="White thin-strap tank top"} with a low or rounded neckline; waistband of gray sweatpants/lounge pants visible. Accessories: Short pearl necklace layered with a thinner metal chain. Pose & Composition: Close-up half-body to waist selfie; torso turned slightly with a slight lean forward; shoulders forming a diagonal line; the subject occupies most of the frame; background slightly blurred. Environment: Indoor setting with a light-colored wall; minimal shelving on the left with small décor items (picture frame / plant / small object); a black chair nearby. Warm indoor lighting with soft shadows, authentic phone-camera texture.
```

## Lo-Fi Gym Mirror Selfie with Sydney Sweeney Likeness

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A mirror selfie taken inside a commercial gym, photographed with a smartphone front camera. The subject is an attractive, athletic woman with a curvy, feminine build, strong thighs and hips, toned legs, and a confident stance. She is wearing a black tank top with a deep neckline and {argument name="shorts color" default="olive-green"} high-waisted workout shorts, white socks, and white trainers.

She stands inside a squat rack, one knee slightly bent, hips angled forward, leaning casually against the barbell. The gym environment is clearly visible: metal racks, weight machines, rubber flooring, high industrial ceiling with exposed beams, warm overhead lights, and other gym-goers blurred in the background.

Her face is fully visible and resembles {argument name="subject likeness" default="Sydney Sweeney"} — soft facial features, expressive eyes, blonde hair styled naturally, minimal makeup, realistic skin texture — captured casually as if mid-workout. Expression is relaxed and confident, not posed.

The photo has a raw smartphone aesthetic:

noticeable film grain and digital noise

low resolution

mild blur and compression artifacts

imperfect focus

uneven indoor lighting

slightly warm color cast

The image feels candid and unpolished, like a real gym mirror selfie posted online.
Photorealistic, casual realism, modern lo-fi aesthetic, natural body proportions.

Camera & Style Cues

Smartphone front camera

Mirror selfie

Slight wide-angle distortion

Indoor gym lighting

No studio polish

Negative Prompt;

ultra-high resolution, professional photoshoot, cinematic lighting, flawless skin, exaggerated curves, plastic look, studio background, cartoon style, text, watermark
```

## Candid Gen Z Fit Check Mirror Selfie Narrative

> Retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Sloane is ten minutes late and just realized the vintage micro-mini dress she bought needs an industrial zipper pull. Panicked, she’s contorting herself in the full-length mirror, using her iPhone to get a full-body fit check before she tries to wrestle the zip the rest of the way up. She knows the lighting in her cramped, cluttered closet corner is terrible, but the photo is just for her group chat to confirm the look is 'giving.' The intensity in her eyes is pure adrenaline, not posing.
```
